/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_de_util.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"import os\nimport importlib.util\nimport csv\nimport math\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\nfrom config import set_global_seed, SEED, MODEL_NAME\n\n# Set global seed at module load time\nset_global_seed()\n\n\ndef load_opt_vals():\n    \"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'dataset.csv')\n    opt_vals = {}\n    if os.path.exists(dataset_path):\n        with open(dataset_path, 'r') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    \n    Args:\n        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    \n    Returns:\n        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    import cocoex\n\n    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    \n    # Create or use provided controller\n    if controller is None:\n        # load meta learning implementations\n        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n    \n    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _reflect(x, lb, ub):\n        \"\"\"Reflect out-of-bound coordinates back into [lb, ub].\n\n        This is often better than simple clipping for BBOB functions because\n        it preserves some of the momentum/direction of the point.\n        \"\"\"\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n        lo = np.asarray(lb, dtype=float)\n        hi = np.asarray(ub, dtype=float)\n        width = hi - lo\n        # Reflect points that are too low\n        below = x < lo\n        x = np.where(below, 2.0 * lo - x, x)\n        # Reflect points that are too high\n        above = x > hi\n        x = np.where(above, 2.0 * hi - x, x)\n        # Safety clamp (handles pathological cases where the range is tiny)\n        return np.clip(x, lo, hi)\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - Latin Hypercube Sampling for better coverage\n        if pop_size > 1:\n            lhs = np.zeros((pop_size, self.dim))\n            for j in range(self.dim):\n                perm = rng.permutation(pop_size)\n                lhs[:, j] = (perm + rng.random(pop_size)) / pop_size\n            pop = lb + lhs * (ub - lb)\n        else:\n            pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = Train every update_freq steps\n   187\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   188\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   189\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   190\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   191\t\n   192\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   193\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   194\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   195\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   196\t\n   197\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   198\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   199\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   200\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   201\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   202\t\n   203\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   204\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   205\t\n   206\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   207\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   208\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   209\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   210\t\n   211\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   212\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   213\t        loss.backward()\n   214\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   216\t\n   217\t        # Decay epsilon\n   221\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   222\t\n   223\t        return loss.item()\n   224\t\n   225\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   226\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   227\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   228\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   229\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   230\t        self.reward_history = []\n   231\t\n   232\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   233\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   234\t        return self.loss_history\n   235\t\n   236\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   237\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   238\t        return self.reward_history\n   239\t\n   240\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   241\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   242\t        return self.total_loss\n   243\t\n   244\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   245\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   246\t        return self.total_reward\n   247\t\n   248\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   249\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   250\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   251\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   252\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   253\t\n   254\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   255\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   256\t\n   257\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   258\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 3  # F, CR, pbest_p\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            try:\n    70\t                self.load(model_path)\n    71\t            except Exception:\n    72\t                # Ignore incompatible old model files; use freshly initialized network\n    73\t                pass\n    74\t\n    75\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    76\t        return None\n    77\t\n    78\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    79\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    80\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    81\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    82\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    83\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    84\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    85\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    86\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    87\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    88\t        else:\n    89\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    90\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    91\t        \n    92\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    93\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    94\t        \n    95\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    96\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    97\t            try:\n    98\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    99\t            except Exception:\n   100\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n   101\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n   102\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n   103\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   104\t\n   105\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   106\t\n   107\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   108\t        try:\n   109\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   110\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   111\t        except Exception:\n   112\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   113\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   114\t        \n   115\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   140\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   141\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   142\t        else:\n   143\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   144\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   145\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   146\t\n   147\t        if self.training:\n   148\t            # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   182\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   183\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   184\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   185\t\n   186\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   187\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   188\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   189\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   190\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   191\t\n   192\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   193\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   194\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   195\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   196\t\n   197\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   198\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   199\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   200\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   201\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   202\t\n   203\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   204\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   205\t\n   206\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   207\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   208\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   209\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   210\t\n   211\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   212\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   213\t        loss.backward()\n   214\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   216\t\n   217\t        # Update target network periodically\n   218\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   219\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   220\t            # Decay epsilon\n   221\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   222\t\n   223\t        return loss.item()\n   224\t\n   225\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   226\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   227\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   228\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   229\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   230\t        self.reward_history = []\n   231\t\n   232\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   233\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   234\t        return self.loss_history\n   235\t\n   236\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   237\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   238\t        return self.reward_history\n   239\t\n   240\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   241\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   242\t        return self.total_loss\n   243\t\n   244\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   245\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   246\t        return self.total_reward\n   247\t\n   248\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   249\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   250\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   251\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   252\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   253\t\n   254\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   255\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   256\t\n   257\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   258\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

It can load/save a model\nand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\nimport os\nimport numpy as np\ntry:\n    import torch\n    import torch.nn as nn\n    import torch.nn.functional as F\nexcept Exception:\n    torch = None\n\nfrom param_controller import ParamController\n\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n\nif torch is not None:\n    class _Net(nn.Module):\n        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n            super().__init__()\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n            \n            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n\n        def forward(self, x):\n            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n            return self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 3  # F, CR, pbest_p\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                # Ignore incompatible old model files; use freshly initialized network\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n\n        # Map pbest_p (fraction of top individuals used for current-to-pbest/1)\n        # to [0.03, 1.0]; smaller values mean more aggressive exploitation\n        try:\n            p_raw = float(out[2])\n            p_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-p_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            p_sig = 0.2\n        pbest_p = 0.03 + (1.0 - 0.03) * p_sig\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'pbest_p': float(pbest_p)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n        # pbest_p controls the size of the elite set used in current-to-pbest/1\n        self.PBEST_vals = np.linspace(0.05, 0.5, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                for pp in self.PBEST_vals:\n                    self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri), 'pbest_p': float(pp)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n        else:\n            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n\n        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n        else:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n\n        if self.training:\n            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n            self._last_obs = observation\n            self._last_action_idx = idx\n            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n\n        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n\n    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n        self.total_reward += reward\n        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n        \n        if not self.training:\n            return\n\n        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n        transition = (\"\n    }\n    </json>",
    "text": "Here are the available tools:\n```\n{'name': 'bash', 'description': 'Run commands in a bash shell\\n* When invoking this tool, the contents of the \"command\" parameter does NOT need to be XML-escaped.\\n* You don\\'t have access to the internet via this tool.\\n* You do have access to a mirror of common linux and python packages via apt and pip.\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user.\\n* To inspect a particular line range of a file, e.g. lines 10-25, try \\'sed -n 10,25p /path/to/the/file\\'.\\n* Please avoid commands that may produce a very large amount of output.\\n* Please run long lived commands in the background, e.g. \\'sleep 10 &\\' or start a server in the background.', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'description': 'The bash command to run.'}}, 'required': ['command']}}\n\n{'name': 'editor', 'description': 'Custom editing tool for viewing, creating and editing files\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user\\n* If `path` is a file, `view` displays the result of applying `cat -n`. If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Be mindful of whitespaces!\\n* If the `old_str` parameter is not unique in the file, the replacement will not be performed. Make sure to include enough context in `old_str` to make it unique\\n* The `new_str` parameter should contain the edited lines that should replace the `old_str`', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'enum': ['view', 'create', 'str_replace', 'insert', 'undo_edit'], 'description': 'The commands to run. Allowed options are: `view`, `create`, `str_replace`, `insert`, `undo_edit`.'}, 'file_text': {'description': 'Required parameter of `create` command, with the content of the file to be created.', 'type': 'string'}, 'insert_line': {'description': 'Required parameter of `insert` command. The `new_str` will be inserted AFTER the line `insert_line` of `path`.', 'type': 'integer'}, 'new_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the new string. Required parameter of `insert` command containing the string to insert.', 'type': 'string'}, 'old_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the string in `path` to replace.', 'type': 'string'}, 'path': {'description': 'Absolute path to file or directory, e.g. `/repo/file.py` or `/repo`.', 'type': 'string'}, 'view_range': {'description': 'Optional parameter of `view` command when `path` points to a file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"sed -n '1,280p' /hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"\"\"\"Meta-learning based ParamController for Differential Evolution.\n\nProvides `DQNController` and `DiscreteDQNController` implementing the `ParamController` interface.\nThe controller wraps a small PyTorch network that maps observations to\nDE parameter values (F, CR, strategy). It can load/save a model\nand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\nimport os\nimport numpy as np\ntry:\n    import torch\n    import torch.nn as nn\n    import torch.nn.functional as F\nexcept Exception:\n    torch = None\n\nfrom param_controller import ParamController\n\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n\nif torch is not None:\n    class _Net(nn.Module):\n        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n            super().__init__()\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n            \n            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n            if seed is not None:\n                self._init_weights(seed)\n\n        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n            g = torch.Generator()\n            g.manual_seed(seed)\n            \n            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n\n        def forward(self, x):\n            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n            return self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 3  # F, CR, pbest_p\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                # Ignore incompatible old model files; use freshly initialized network\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n\n        # Map pbest_p (fraction of top individuals used for current-to-pbest/1)\n        # to [0.03, 1.0]; smaller values mean more aggressive exploitation\n        try:\n            p_raw = float(out[2])\n            p_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-p_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            p_sig = 0.2\n        pbest_p = 0.03 + (1.0 - 0.03) * p_sig\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'pbest_p': float(pbest_p)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n        # pbest_p controls the size of the elite set used in current-to-pbest/1\n        self.PBEST_vals = np.linspace(0.05, 0.5, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                for pp in self.PBEST_vals:\n                    self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri), 'pbest_p': float(pp)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # linear population size reduction\n   177\t            if iteration > 1 and pop_size > 4:\n   178\t                target_size = max(4, int(self.pop_size * (1.0 - 0.6 * progress)))\n   179\t                if target_size < pop_size:\n   180\t                    keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_size]\n   181\t                    pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n   182\t                    fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n   183\t                    pop_size = int(target_size)\n   184\t                    archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 20)\n   185\t\n   186\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   187\t            pop_old = pop.copy()\n   188\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   189\t            S_F = []\n   190\t            S_CR = []\n   191\t            F_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   192\t            CR_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   193\t            \n   194\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   195\t            pbest_num = max(1, int(pop_size * pbest_p))\n   196\t            pbest_indices = sorted_idx[:pbest_num]\n   197\t            \n   198\t            # Generate trial vectors using SHADE current-to-pbest/1\n   199\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   200\t                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution based on history memory\n   201\t                mi = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n   202\t                mu_F = float(memory_F[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_F * 0.5\n   203\t                mu_CR = float(memory_CR[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_CR * 0.5\n   204\t                F_i = float(mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\n   205\t                if F_i <= 0.0:\n   206\t                    F_i = 0.1\n   207\t                elif F_i > 1.0:\n   208\t                    F_i = 1.0\n   209\t                CR_i = float(rng.normal(mu_CR, 0.1))\n   210\t                CR_i = max(0.0, min(1.0, CR_i))\n   211\t                F_i_values[i] = F_i\n   212\t                CR_i_values[i] = CR_i\n   213\t                \n   214\t                # Choose pbest from top pbest_p individuals\n   215\t                pbest_i = int(pbest_indices[int(rng.randint(len(pbest_indices)))])\n   216\t                pbest_x = pop[pbest_i]\n   217\t                \n   218\t                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   219\t                base = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest_x - pop[i])\n   220\t                \n   221\t                # r1 from population, r2 from population or archive\n   222\t                r1 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n   223\t                while r1 == i:\n   224\t                    r1 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n   225\t                candidate_r2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n   226\t                if len(archive) > 0:\n   227\t                    candidate_r2 += [pop_size + j for j in range(len(archive))]\n   228\t                if len(candidate_r2) < 1:\n   229\t                    r2 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n   230\t                    x2 = pop[r2]\n   231\t                else:\n   232\t                    r2 = int(candidate_r2[int(rng.randint(len(candidate_r2)))])\n   233\t                    if r2 >= pop_size:\n   234\t                        x2 = archive[r2 - pop_size]\n   235\t                    else:\n   236\t                        x2 = pop[r2]\n   237\t                \n   238\t                mutant = base + F_i * (pop[r1] - x2)\n   239\t                \n   240\t                # Binomial crossover\n   241\t                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\n   242\t                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n   243\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   244\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   245\t                trial = self._reflect(trial, lb, ub)\n   246\t                new_pop[i] = trial\n   247\t            \n   248\t            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\n   249\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   250\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   251\t                    done = True\n   252\t                    break\n   253\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   254\t                evals += 1\n   255\t                \n   256\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   257\t                    # Add previous position to archive before replacement\n   258\t                    old_x = pop_old[i].reshape(1, -1)\n   259\t                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n   260\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, old_x])\n   261\t                    else:\n   262\t                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(archive_capacity))\n   263\t                        archive[replace_idx] = old_x\n   264\t                    \n   265\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   266\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   267\t                    S_F.append(F_i_values[i])\n   268\t                    S_CR.append(CR_i_values[i])\n   269\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   270\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   271\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   272\t            \n   273\t            # Update SHADE memory with successful parameters (weighted Lehmer mean for F)\n   274\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   275\t                S_F_arr = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\n   276\t                S_CR_arr = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\n   277\t                sum_F = float(np.sum(S_F_arr))\n   278\t                if sum_F > 0:\n   279\t                    memory_F[memory_idx] = float(np.sum(S_F_arr * S_F_arr) / sum_F)\n   280\t                else:\n   281\t                    memory_F[memory_idx] = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\n   282\t                memory_CR[memory_idx] = float(np.mean(S_CR_arr))\n   283\t                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % memory_size\n   284\t\n   285\t            # Local search around the current best solution to accelerate convergence\n   286\t            if (iteration >= local_search_interval and iteration % local_search_interval == 0\n   287\t                    and evals < self.max_evals):\n   288\t                # Perturbation scale decreases as the run progresses\n   289\t                sigma_local = (ub - lb) * max(1e-7, 0.01 * (1.0 - 0.5 * progress))\n   290\t                for _ in range(local_search_n):\n   291\t                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   292\t                        break\n   293\t                    cand = self._reflect(gbest + sigma_local * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n   294\t                    cand_f = evaluate_problem(problem, cand)\n   295\t                    evals += 1\n   296\t                    if cand_f < gbest_f:\n   297\t                        gbest_f = cand_f\n   298\t                        gbest = cand.copy()\n   299\t                        # Replace the worst individual with this improved point\n   300\t                        worst_i = int(np.argmax(fitness))\n   301\t                        pop[worst_i] = cand.copy()\n   302\t                        fitness[worst_i] = cand_f\n   303\t\n   304\t            # Stagnation detection: restart worst individuals if no improvement for several iterations\n   305\t            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\n   306\t                stagnation_count = 0\n   307\t            else:\n   308\t                stagnation_count += 1\n   309\t            \n   310\t            if stagnation_count >= 5:\n   311\t                n_restart = max(1, int(pop_size * 0.15))\n   312\t                worst_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[-n_restart:]\n   313\t                sigma = (ub - lb) * 0.02 * (1.0 + progress)\n   314\t                for idx in worst_idx:\n   315\t                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   316\t                        break\n   317\t                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n   318\t                        pop[idx] = self._reflect(gbest + sigma * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n   319\t                    else:\n   320\t                        pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\n   321\t                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\n   322\t                    evals += 1\n   323\t                    if fitness[idx] < gbest_f:\n   324\t                        gbest_f = fitness[idx]\n   325\t                        gbest = pop[idx].copy()\n   326\t                stagnation_count = 0\n   327\t            \n   328\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   329\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   330\t            \n   331\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   332\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   333\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   334\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   335\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   336\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   337\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   338\t            \n   339\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   340\t\n   341\t        self.stats = {\n   342\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   343\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   344\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   345\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   346\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   347\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   348\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   349\t        }\n   350\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\n     5\tobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\n     6\tadjustment (F, CR).\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\t\n     9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = None\n    16\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    17\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    18\t\n    19\t\n    20\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n    42\t                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n    43\t                 swarm_size: int = None):\n    44\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    45\t        if lower_bounds is not None:\n    46\t            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    47\t            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    48\t                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n    49\t        else:\n    50\t            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    51\t        \n    52\t        if upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t        selected = []\n    71\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    72\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    73\t            selected.append(idx)\n    74\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    @staticmethod\n    81\t    def _reflect(x, lb, ub):\n    82\t        \"\"\"Reflect out-of-bound coordinates back into [lb, ub].\n    83\t\n    84\t        This is often better than simple clipping for BBOB functions because\n    85\t        it preserves some of the momentum/direction of the point.\n    86\t        \"\"\"\n    87\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    88\t        lo = np.asarray(lb, dtype=float)\n    89\t        hi = np.asarray(ub, dtype=float)\n    90\t        width = hi - lo\n    91\t        # Reflect points that are too low\n    92\t        below = x < lo\n    93\t        x = np.where(below, 2.0 * lo - x, x)\n    94\t        # Reflect points that are too high\n    95\t        above = x > hi\n    96\t        x = np.where(above, 2.0 * hi - x, x)\n    97\t        # Safety clamp (handles pathological cases where the range is tiny)\n    98\t        return np.clip(x, lo, hi)\n    99\t\n   100\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n   101\t        if self.controller is not None:\n   102\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n   103\t\n   104\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n   105\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n   106\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n   107\t        \n   108\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n   109\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Initialize population - Latin Hypercube Sampling for better coverage\n   112\t        if pop_size > 1:\n   113\t            lhs = np.zeros((pop_size, self.dim))\n   114\t            for j in range(self.dim):\n   115\t                perm = rng.permutation(pop_size)\n   116\t                lhs[:, j] = (perm + rng.random(pop_size)) / pop_size\n   117\t            pop = lb + lhs * (ub - lb)\n   118\t        else:\n   119\t            pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n   120\t        \n   121\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   122\t        evals = 0\n   123\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   124\t        \n   125\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   126\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   127\t                break\n   128\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   129\t            evals += 1\n   130\t\n   131\t        # Track best\n   132\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   133\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   134\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   135\t        \n   136\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   137\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   138\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   139\t\n   140\t        # Main loop\n   141\t        iteration = 0\n   142\t        done = False\n   143\t        \n   144\t        # SHADE (Success-History Adaptive Differential Evolution) state\n   145\t        memory_size = 10\n   146\t        memory_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\n   147\t        memory_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\n   148\t        memory_idx = 0\n   149\t        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n   150\t        archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 20)\n   151\t        stagnation_count = 0\n   152\t        local_search_interval = 20\n   153\t        local_search_n = max(1, min(3, pop_size // 10))\n   154\t        \n   155\t        while not done:\n   156\t            iteration += 1\n   157\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   158\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   159\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   160\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   161\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   162\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   163\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   164\t            \n   165\t            # Optional controller override (still supported, but SHADE also adapts internally)\n   166\t            ctrl_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   167\t            ctrl_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   168\t            \n   169\t            progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, self.max_evals))\n   170\t            # More exploitation as the budget is consumed\n   171\t            pbest_p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n   172\t            # Allow the controller to override the pbest selection pressure\n   173\t            if 'pbest_p' in params:\n   174\t                pbest_p = float(np.clip(float(params['pbest_p']), 0.01, 1.0))\n   175\t\n   176\t            # L-SHADE style linear population size reduction\n   177\t            if iteration > 1 and pop_size > 4:\n   178\t                target_size = max(4, int(self.pop_size * (1.0 - 0.6 * progress)))\n   179\t                if target_size < pop_size:\n   180\t                    keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_size]\n   181\t                    pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n   182\t                    fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n   183\t                    pop_size = int(target_size)\n   184\t                    archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 20)\n   185\t\n   186\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   187\t            pop_old = pop.copy()\n   188\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   189\t            S_F = []\n   190\t            S_CR = []\n   191\t            F_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   192\t            CR_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   193\t            \n   194\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   195\t            pbest_num = max(1, int(pop_size * pbest_p))\n   196\t            pbest_indices = sorted_idx[:pbest_num]\n   197\t            \n   198\t            # Generate trial vectors using SHADE current-to-pbest/1\n   199\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   200\t                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution based on history memory\n   201\t                mi = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n   202\t                mu_F = float(memory_F[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_F * 0.5\n   203\t                mu_CR = float(memory_CR[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_CR * 0.5\n   204\t                F_i = float(mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\n   205\t                if F_i <= 0.0:\n   206\t                    F_i = 0.1\n   207\t                elif F_i > 1.0:\n   208\t                    F_i = 1.0\n   209\t                CR_i =
